This projection approach robust. Need compute full gradient for h (length n). We can implement gradient_full(h, c, weights, n):
Initialize g zeros n.
For each idx lag l from 0 to n-1? Let's use positive lags k=0..n-1.
Indices in full: pos_idx = n-1+k, neg_idx = n-1-k. c_pos=c[pos], c_neg=c[neg]. weights w_pos,w_neg.
If k==0: g += w0*(1 - 2*h)
else:
 # contribution from lag +k (P)
 # i>=k: 1 - h[i-k]
 g[k:] += w_pos*(1 - h[:n-k])
 # i<n-k: -h[i+k]
 g[:n-k] += -w_pos*h[k:]
 # lag -k (N)
 # i<n-k: 1 - h[i+k]
 g[:n-k] += w_neg*(1 - h[k:])
 # i>=k: -h[i-k]
 g[k:] += -w_neg*h[:n-k]
This matches P and N. Complexity loop k=1..n-1, each slicing O(n) => O(n^2) Python overhead n times but each slice C. For n=1000, 1000 iterations per gradient; each 4 slice ops of length ~n, total ~4e6 element operations plus Python loop 1000. 100 gradients = 400M element ops + 100k Python loops maybe high but acceptable? Could be several seconds to tens. Need speed.

```
def fft_corr(a,b):
    n=len(a); m=len(b)
    N=1
    while N < n+m-1: N <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N); fb=np.fft.rfft(b[::-1],N)
    return np.real(np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,N))[:n+m-1]
```

Optimization scheduler:
```
def optimize_lse_z(z,n,deadline, alphas, maxiters):
   best_z=z; best_val=...
   for alpha in alphas:
      if time > deadline-30: break
      options={'maxiter': maxiters, 'ftol':1e-15, 'gtol':1e-10, 'maxcor':50}
      res=minimize(func, z, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options=options)
      z=res.x; val=func(z)
      if val<best: ...
      # maybe do line search if improved?
   return best_z, best_h
```

```
def choose_feasible(L,R,val):
  if val < -1e-12: return None
  if val >= 0.25:
    if L>R: return None
    return (L+R)/2
  disc=1-4*val
  r1=(1-sqrt(disc))/2; r2=(1+sqrt(disc))/2
  segs=[(0,r1),(r2,1)]
  best=None
  for a,b in segs:
    la=max(L,a); rb=min(R,b)
    if la <= rb+1e-14:
       # choose midpoint, maybe closer to current? just mid
       return (la+rb)/2
  return None
```
For binary search feasibility, we can implement `feasible_T(T,L/R?)` but recompute L/R each time. To speed, precompute non_diag arrays and base. Then per T:
```
L=0; R=1; any constraints
if unaff_max > T+eps: return False
if len(a)>0:
  pos=a>1e-14; neg=a<-1e-14
  if any(pos): R=min(R, np.min((T-base[pos])/a[pos]))
  if any(neg): L=max(L, np.max((T-base[neg])/a[neg]))
  if L>R+1e-12: return False
return choose_feasible(L,R,val) is not None
```
Then after binary, choose x. If no x due numerical, return h0.

Define `make_antisym(h,n)`:
```
m=n//2
if even:
 z = 0.5*(h[:m] + 1 - h[m:][::-1])
 z = clip
 h2 = build_h(z)
else:
 z = 0.5*(h[:m] + 1 - h[m+1:][::-1])
 h2 = build; h2[m]=0.5
h2 = np.clip
return h2
```
Sum auto. This is better than previous 0.5(h+1-h_rev) because pair sum exact in float? h2 built from z.

Let's design `lse_obj_grad` carefully.

```
def lse_obj_grad(z, n, alpha):
    m = n//2
    x = 1.0/(1.0+np.exp(-np.clip(z,-50,50)))
    h = np.empty(n)
    if n % 2 == 0:
        h[:m] = x
        h[m:] = 1.0 - x[::-1]
    else:
        h[:m] = x
        h[m] = 0.5
        h[m+1:] = 1.0 - x[::-1]
    h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    v = 1.0 - h
    # FFT size
    N = 1
    need = 2*n - 1
    while N < need: N <<= 1
    fh = np.fft.rfft(h, N)
    c = np.real(np.fft.irfft(fh * np.fft.rfft(v[::-1], N), N)[:need])
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)  # maybe small negative
    dx = 2.0/n
    cmax = float(c.max())
    # If cmax ==0? not
    shift = alpha * dx * (c - cmax)
    # Numerical: shift <=0, maybe min -inf
    # Use max? max 0. If shift has nan? clip
    shift = np.clip(shift, -800.0, 0.0) # exp underflow avoided; but clipping changes weights for far peaks negligible
    expv = np.exp(shift)
    sw = expv.sum()
    if sw <= 0 or not np.isfinite(sw):
       # fallback one-hot max? set gradient approximate? But shouldn't.
       w = np.zeros(need); w[c.max()>0]=1? 
    else: w=expv/sw
    lse = cmax + np.log(sw)/alpha
    w0 = float(w[n-1])
    pos = np.zeros(n)
    neg = np.zeros(n)
    if n > 1:
       pos[1:] = w[n:]
       neg[1:] = w[:n-1][::-1]
    pos_pref = np.cumsum(pos)
    neg_pref = np.cumsum(neg)[::-1]
    lin = w0 + pos_pref + neg_pref
    T = pos + neg
    fT = np.fft.rfft(T, N)
    left = np.real(np.fft.irfft(fT * fh, N))[:n]
    TR = T[::-1]
    fTR = np.fft.rfft(TR, N)
    right = np.real(np.fft.irfft(fTR * fh, N))[n-1:n-1+n]
    B = left + right
    g = lin - 2.0*w0*h - B
    # small numerical clip
    g = np.clip(g, -1e12, 1e12)
    if n % 2 == 0:
       dz = dx * (g[:m] - g[m:][::-1]) * (x*(1.0-x))
    else:
       dz = dx * (g[:m] - g[m+1:][::-1]) * (x*(1.0-x))
    # If some nans/inf, set zero
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(dz)):
       dz = np.nan_to_num(dz, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
       lse = float(np.nan_to_num(lse, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0))
    return lse, dz
```

`exact_update` code:
```
def exact_update(h,c,p,n):
  h0=h[p]
  L_total=2*n-1
  lmin = min(p-(n-1), -p)
  lmax = max(p, n-1-p)
  mlo = n-1+lmin; mhi=n-1+lmax
  if mlo<0: mlo=0
  if mhi>=L_total: mhi=L_total-1
  ms=np.arange(mlo,mhi+1)
  l=ms-(n-1)
  A=np.zeros(len(ms))
  v1 = (p-l >=0) & (p-l < n)
  if np.any(v1): A[v1] += 1.0 - h[p-l[v1]]
  v2 = (p+l >=0) & (p+l < n)
  if np.any(v2): A[v2] -= h[p+l[v2]]
  diag_mask = (l==0)
  base_all = c[ms]
  if np.any(diag_mask):
      diag_idx = np.where(diag_mask)[0][0]
      A[diag_idx]=0.0
      base0 = base_all[diag_idx]
      self_base = h0*(1-h0)
  else: base0 = None; self_base=0
  mask = (np.abs(A)>1e-14) | diag_mask
  # U
  U=0.0
  if mlo>0: U=max(U, float(np.max(c[:mlo])))
  if mhi+1<L_total: U=max(U, float(np.max(c[mhi+1:])))
  if np.any(~mask): U=max(U, float(np.max(base_all[~mask])))
  cur=float(np.max(c))
  if cur <= U + 1e-15: return h0
  a_nd=np.ones(len(ms),dtype=bool); a_nd[diag_mask]=False; a_nd &= np.abs(A)>1e-14
  base_nd=base_all[a_nd]; a_nd_arr=A[a_nd]
  lo=U; hi=cur
  for _ in range(38):
     T=(lo+hi)*0.5
     if exact_feasible(T,U,base_nd,a_nd_arr,base0,h0): hi=T
     else: lo=T
     if hi-lo<1e-14: break
  T=(lo+hi)*0.5
  # compute bounds and choose
  xlo=0; xhi=1
  if len(base_nd)>0:
     pos=a_nd_arr>1e-14; neg=a_nd_arr<-1e-14
     if np.any(pos): xhi=min(xhi, float(np.min((T-base_nd[pos])/a_nd_arr[pos])))
     if np.any(neg): xlo=max(xlo, float(np.max((T-base_nd[neg])/a_nd_arr[neg])))
  xlo=max(xlo,0); xhi=min(xhi,1)
  if base0 is None:
     if xlo>xhi+1e-12: return h0
     return float(np.clip((xlo+xhi)/2,0,1))
  else:
     val = T - base0 + h0*(1-h0)
     x = exact_choose(xlo,xhi,val)
     if x is None:
        # maybe choose h0 if feasible? compute max with h0? return h0
        return h0
     return float(np.clip(x,0,1))
```

def exact_choose(L,R,val):
 if val < -1e-12: return None
 if val >= 0.25 - 1e-15:
   if L > R + 1e-12: return None
   return (L+R)*0.5
 d=1-4*val

def coord_descent(h,n,max_passes,deadline,antisym=False,nbisect=38):
 h=h.copy()

   if time>deadline: break

   cur=compute_c5? We can compute direct rarely. cfft max.

   for p in order:

      if abs(xnew-h[p])>1e-13: h[p]=xnew; improved=True
   if antisym: h=make_antisym(h,n)
   else: h=project_feasible(h,n)

   pass
 return h, compute_c5(h)

Need `project_feasible` efficient. We can use simple:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
 h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 target=n/2
 for _ in range(100):
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
   if diff>0:
      room=1-h
      tot=room.sum()
      if tot<1e-15: break
      h=np.clip(h + (diff/tot)*room,0,1)
   else:
      tot=h.sum()
      if tot<1e-15: break
      h=np.clip(h + (diff/tot)*h,0,1)
 return h
```
This may not exact but okay.

`make_antisym`:
```
def make_antisym(h,n):
 z=np.empty(n//2)
 if n%2==0:
   z=0.5*(h[:m]+1-h[m:][::-1])
 else:
   z=0.5*(h[:m]+1-h[m+1:][::-1])
 z=np.clip(z,0,1)
 return build_antisym(z,n)
```
Sum auto but floating. Could adjust z to ensure direct sum? Not necessary.

`build_antisym(z,n)` as above. For n even, h[m:]=1-z[::-1]. For n odd, center 0.5.

```

 try:
   h0=np.array(initial_h_values,float).copy()
 except: h0=None

   n=700; x=...; h0=...
 else:
   n=len(h0)
   if n%2==1: n+=1? We can interpolate to n even maybe.
 # Ensure h0 in [0,1]
 h0=np.clip(h0,0,1)
 h0=project_feasible(h0,n)
 # Make antisym start
 hA=make_antisym(h0,n)
 best_h=hA; best_c=compute_c5(hA)
 # Possibly if full h0 better? But we will full optimize.
 h_full=project_feasible(h0,n); cf=compute_c5(h_full); if cf<best_c: best_h=h_full
 # L-BFGS on anti-sym n
 n_anti=n if even else n+1? Use n0 maybe if even. If n0 odd, set n_even=2*int(n0/2) or n0+1. Use n_target list.
 for n_try in [n_even, maybe max(500,int(0.8*n_even)), min(1000,int(1.2*n_even))] unique:
   if time > deadline-100: break

   z,hz = lse_optimize_z(z,n_try,deadline, alpha schedule)

Let's implement `interp_make_antisym(h,n_new)`:
```
def interpolate_to(h,n_new):
 x_old=np.linspace(0,2,len(h)); x_new=np.linspace(0,2,n_new); return np.clip(np.interp(x_new,x_old,h),0,1)
```
Then make_antisym.

`z_from_h(h,n)` for anti-sym:
```
m=n//2
if even: z=0.5*(h[:m]+1-h[m:][::-1])
else: z=0.5*(h[:m]+1-h[m+1:][::-1])
z=np.log(np.clip(z,1e-9,1-1e-9)+(1-...)? )
return np.clip(z,-20,20)
```

`lse_optimize_n(z,n,deadline,alphas,max_step=40,refine=True)`:

Let's detail components:
```
def lse_from_c(c,n,alpha):
    dx=2.0/n
    cmax_unit=float(c.max())
    Cmax=cmax_unit*dx
    shift=alpha*dx*(c-cmax_unit)
    shift=np.clip(shift, -700.0, 0.0)
    ev=np.exp(shift)
    sw=float(ev.sum())
    if sw <= 0 or not finite: w=np.zeros_like(c); idx=int(np.argmax(c)); w[idx]=1.0
    else: w=ev/sw
    lse=Cmax + math.log(sw)/alpha
    return float(lse), w
```
Use `math.log`.

`gradient_from_weights(h,w,n)`:
```
N=1; need=2*n-1; while N<need: N<<=1
fh=np.fft.rfft(h,N)
pos=np.zeros(n); neg=np.zeros(n)
w0=w[n-1]
if n>1:
 pos[1:]=w[n:]
 neg[1:]=w[:n-1][::-1]
lin=w0 + np.cumsum(pos) + np.cumsum(neg)[::-1]
T=pos+neg
fT=np.fft.rfft(T,N); left=irfft(fT*fh,N)[:n]
fTR=np.fft.rfft(T[::-1],N); right=irfft(fTR*fh,N)[n-1:n-1+n]
g=lin - 2*w0*h - (left+right)
return g
```
Need if n=1? not.

Check dimensions: `pos[1:] = w[n:]` length n-1, full indices n..2n-2. `neg[1:] = w[:n-1][::-1]` length n-1: full indices n-2 down to0. Good.

This gradient formula uses full weights for c (not scaled by dx). `lin` as before. For n=1, pos length1.

```
def full_lse_pen_obj(h,n,alpha,rho):
 c=correlate_c_fft(h)
 lse,w=lse_from_c(c,n,alpha)
 g=gradient_from_weights(h,w,n)
 dx=2.0/n
 diff=h.sum()-0.5*n
 obj=lse + 0.5*rho*diff*diff
 grad=dx*g + rho*diff*np.ones(n)
 return obj,grad
```

Implement `coord_descent_polish(h,n,deadline,passes=4,antisym=False,rng=None)`:
```
h=np.clip(h.copy(),0,1); h=project_feasible if not antisym else make_antisym
best_h=h; best_c=compute_c5(h); c_before=best_c
for pass in range(passes):
 if time>deadline-5: break
 h_work=h.copy(); h_before=h.copy(); c=correlate_c_fft(h_work)
 improved=False
 order=rng.permutation(n)
 for p in order:
   if time>deadline-5: break
   x=exact_update(h_work,c,p,n)
   if abs(x-h_work[p])>1e-13:
      h_work[p]=x
      c=correlate_c_fft(h_work)
      improved=True
 if antisym: h_work=make_antisym(h_work,n)
 else: h_work=project_feasible(h_work,n)
 cw=compute_c5(h_work)
 if cw < c_before + 1e-12:
    h=c? h_work; c_before=cw
 if cw < best_c: best...
 if not improved or cw > c_before -1e-13? break
 return best_h,best_c
```

Now, exact_update code details:
```
def exact_update(h,c,p,n):
    h0=float(h[p]); L=2*n-1
    lmin = min(p-(n-1), -p)
    lmax = max(p, n-1-p)
    mlo = n-1 + lmin; mhi = n-1 + lmax
    if mlo < 0: mlo=0
    if mhi >= L: mhi=L-1
    ms = np.arange(mlo, mhi+1)
    lags = ms - (n-1)
    A = np.zeros(len(ms), dtype=float)
    idx1 = p - lags
    v1 = (idx1 >= 0) & (idx1 < n)
    if np.any(v1): A[v1] += 1.0 - h[idx1[v1]]
    idx2 = p + lags
    v2 = (idx2 >= 0) & (idx2 < n)
    if np.any(v2): A[v2] -= h[idx2[v2]]
    diag = (lags == 0)
    base_all = c[ms]
    if np.any(diag):
       didx=int(np.argmax(diag)) # since one true
       A[didx]=0.0
       base0=float(base_all[didx])
       self_base=h0*(1.0-h0)
    else:
       base0=None; self_base=0.0
    mask = (np.abs(A) > 1e-14) | diag
    U=0.0
    if mlo > 0: U=max(U, float(np.max(c[:mlo])))
    if mhi + 1 < L: U=max(U, float(np.max(c[mhi+1:])))
    if np.any(~mask): U=max(U, float(np.max(base_all[~mask])))
    cur=float(np.max(c))
    if cur <= U + 1e-15: return h0
    nd = np.ones(len(ms), dtype=bool); nd[diag]=False
    a_all = A[nd & (np.abs(A)>1e-14)]? Need base correspond.
```
Create linear mask:
```
lin_mask = nd & (np.abs(A)>1e-14)
base_lin=base_all[lin_mask]; a_lin=A[lin_mask]
pos = a_lin > 1e-14; neg = a_lin < -1e-14
base_pos=base_lin[pos]; a_pos=a_lin[pos]; base_neg=base_lin[neg]; a_neg=a_lin[neg]
```

  # maybe choose nearest h0: x=min(max(h0,Lx),Rx)

val=(T-base0+self_base) # Wait self_base is h0(1-h0), yes T-base0+h0(1-h0)

   # midpoint of first feasible?

`correlate_fft`:
```
def corr_fft(a,b):
 n=len(a); need=2*n-1; N=1 << max(1,(need-1).bit_length())
 c=np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(a,N)*np.fft.rfft(b[::-1],N),N)
 return np.real(np.maximum(c[:need],0.0))
```
Note if b length n. Good.

`gradient_from_weights`:
Use same N. We need `np.fft.rfft(h,N)` and `T`. Good.

`z_obj` building h: To avoid clipping each time? `x = np.expit`? `1/(1+exp(-z))`. Use `nz=np.clip(z,-50,50)`. Build. If n even, h = np.concatenate? Avoid allocation? Fine.
```
def z_obj(z,n,alpha):
 m=n//2; nz=np.clip(z,-50,50); x=1/(1+np.exp(-nz)); h=np.empty(n)
 if n&1:
  h[:m]=x; h[m]=0.5; h[m+1:]=1-x[::-1]
 else: h[:m]=x; h[m:]=1-x[::-1]
 h=np.clip(h,0,1); c=corr_fft(h,1-h); lse,w=lse_c(c,n,alpha); g=grad_weights(h,w,n); dx=2/n
 if n&1: dz=dx*(g[:m]-g[m+1:][::-1])*x*(1-x)
 else: dz=dx*(g[:m]-g[m:][::-1])*x*(1-x)
 return lse, np.nan_to_num(dz)
```
For n even, comp index `n-1-i` corresponds array `h[m:]` reversed. Good. For n odd, comp index `n-1-i=m+?`. If n=2m+1, comp of i is n-1-i=2m-i. For i=0 comp 2m = m+(m-1) = m + (m-1?) `h[m+1:][::-1]` length m: first comp index 2m, last m+1. Yes.

`full_obj`:
```
def full_obj(h,n,alpha,rho):
 c=corr_fft(h,1-h); lse,w=lse_c(c,n,alpha); g=grad_weights(h,w,n); dx=2/n; diff=h.sum()-n/2
 return lse+0.5*rho*diff*diff, dx*g + rho*diff*np.ones(n)
```

def lse_grad_from_h(h,n,alpha):
    need=2*n-1; N=...
    fh=np.fft.rfft(h,N)

```

Code:
```
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time, math

def compute_c5(h):
    dx=2.0/len(h)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full'))*dx)

def interpolate_to(h,n):
    if len(h)==n: return np.clip(h,0,1)
    x0=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,len(h)); x1=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
    return np.clip(np.interp(x1,x0,h),0.0,1.0)

def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.clip(h.copy(),0.0,1.0); target=0.5*n
    for _ in range(80):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
       if diff>0:
          room=1.0-h; tot=room.sum()
          if tot<1e-15: break
          h=np.clip(h + (diff/tot)*room,0.0,1.0)
       else:
          tot=h.sum()
          if tot<1e-15: break
          h=np.clip(h + (diff/tot)*h,0.0,1.0)
    return h
...
```

`build_antisym`:
```
def build_antisym(z,n):
 m=n//2; x=1.0/(1.0+np.exp(-np.clip(z,-50,50)))
 h=np.empty(n)
 if n%2==0:
   h[:m]=x; h[m:]=1.0-x[::-1]
 else:
   h[:m]=x; h[m]=0.5; h[m+1:]=1.0-x[::-1]
 return np.clip(h,0,1)
```

   z=0.5*(h[:m]+1.0-h[m:][::-1])
 else:
   z=0.5*(h[:m]+1.0-h[m+1:][::-1])
 z=np.clip(z,0,1)
 return build_antisym(np.log(np.clip(z,1e-9,1-1e-9))+? )
```

`lse_c`:
```
def lse_c(c,n,alpha):
 dx=2.0/n; cmax=float(c.max()); shift=alpha*dx*(c-cmax); shift=np.clip(shift,-700.0,0.0); e=np.exp(shift); s=float(e.sum())
 if s<=0 or not math.isfinite(s):
   w=np.zeros(c.shape); w[c.argmax() if c.size else 0]=1.0
 else: w=e/s
 return float(cmax*dx + math.log(s)/alpha), w
```
Need if c.size=0 no. n≥2.

`grad_weights`:
```
def grad_weights(h,w,n,fh,N):
 w0=float(w[n-1]); pos=np.zeros(n); neg=np.zeros(n)
 if n>1:
  pos[1:]=w[n:]
  neg[1:]=w[:n-1][::-1]
 lin=w0 + np.cumsum(pos) + np.cumsum(neg)[::-1]
 T=pos+neg
 fT=np.fft.rfft(T,N)
 left=np.fft.irfft(fT*fh,N)[:n]
 fTR=np.fft.rfft(T[::-1],N)
 right=np.fft.irfft(fTR*fh,N)[n-1:n-1+n]
 g=lin - 2.0*w0*h - left - right
 return g
```
`np.fft.irfft` returns real, no need `np.real`? It returns real. But may have tiny imaginary? irfft returns real. Good.

`obj_core`:
```
def obj_core(h,n,alpha):
 need=2*n-1; N=1 << max(1,(need-1).bit_length())
 fh=np.fft.rfft(h,N)
 v=1.0-h
 c=np.fft.irfft(fh*np.fft.rfft(v[::-1],N),N)[:need]
 c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
 lse,w=lse_c(c,n,alpha)
 g=grad_weights(h,w,n,fh,N)
 return lse,w,g
```

`z_obj`:
```
def z_obj(z,n,alpha):
 m=n//2; nz=np.clip(z,-50,50); x=1.0/(1.0+np.exp(-nz)); h=build_from_x(x,n)
 lse,w,g=obj_core(h,n,alpha); dx=2.0/n
 if n%2==0:
  dz=dx*(g[:m]-g[m:][::-1])*x*(1.0-x)
 else:
  dz=dx*(g[:m]-g[m+1:][::-1])*x*(1.0-x)
 if not np.all(np.isfinite(dz)): dz=np.nan_to_num(dz)
 return float(lse), dz
```
For n even, h[m:] is reverse comp. For gradient comp index n-1-i corresponds h[m:][::-1]. Good.
For n odd, comp h[m+1:][::-1]. Good.

`full_obj`:
```
def full_obj(h,n,alpha,rho):
 lse,w,g=obj_core(h,n,alpha); dx=2.0/n; diff=float(np.sum(h)-0.5*n); obj=lse+0.5*rho*diff*diff; grad=dx*g + rho*diff*np.ones(n)
 if not finite grad: nan_to_num
 return float(obj), grad
```

`opt_z`:
```
def opt_z(z,n,deadline,alphas):
 m=n//2; bounds=[(-20.0,20.0)]*m
 best_h=build_antisym(z,n); best_c=compute_c5(best_h); best_z=np.asarray(z).copy()
 zc=np.asarray(z).copy()
 options={'maxiter':50,'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-9,'maxcor':20}
 for al in alphas:
   if time.time()>deadline-3: break
   if compute_c5(build_from_x(np.clip(1/(1+np.exp(-np.clip(zc,-50,50))),n)) > best_c + 0.002: zc=best_z.copy()
   for _ in range(10):
     if time.time()>deadline-3: break
     try: res=minimize(z_obj,zc,args=(n,al),jac=True,method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,options=options)
     except Exception: break
     zn=np.clip(res.x,-20,20); hn=build_antisym(zn,n); cn=compute_c5(hn)
     if cn<best_c: best_c=cn; best_h=hn; best_z=zn.copy()
     zc=zn
     if res.status==0 and cn >= best_c-1e-14: break
 return best_h,best_c
```
Avoid repeated build for current c: We can just use `cn` from last chunk; but at alpha start if zc is from previous, maybe. To save, compute `zc_h=build_antisym(zc,n); zc_c=compute_c5(zc_h)`. Fine.

`opt_full` similar.

Need ensure `options` dict reused? `minimize` may modify? It shouldn't. But to be safe create new each call? Use dict each. Okay.

Run details:
```
def run(...):
 t0=time.time(); budget=max(10,int(budget_s)); deadline=t0+budget-5
 rng=np.random.RandomState(seed) # unused maybe okay
 # init
 try: h0=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
 except Exception: h0=None
 if h0 is None or h0.size<20:
   n0=700; x=np.linspace(0,2,n0); h0=0.5+0.35*np.sin(np.pi*x)+0.15*np.sin(2*np.pi*x)
 n0=len(h0)
 base_n = n0 if n0%2==0 else n0+1
 h0=interpolate_to(h0,base_n); h0=project_feasible(h0,base_n)
 h_s=make_antisym(h0,base_n); c_s=compute_c5(h_s); best_h=h_s; best_c=c_s
 c_f=compute_c5(h0); if c_f<best_c: best_h=h0; best_c=c_f
 # z opt main
 if time.time()<deadline-120:
   hz,cz=opt_z(z_from_h(h_s,base_n), base_n, deadline-150, [20,100,500,2000,8000,30000])
   if cz<best_c: best_h,best_c=hz,cz
 # sym candidate
 h_sy=make_antisym(best_h,base_n); cs=compute_c5(h_sy); if cs<best_c: best_h,best_c=h_sy,cs
 # full penalty base
 if time.time()<deadline-80:
   hf,cf=opt_full(best_h,base_n, deadline-60, [100,500,2000,10000], 1500.0)
   if cf<best_c: best_h,best_c=hf,cf
 # sym final
 h_sy=make_antisym(best_h,base_n); cs=compute_c5(h_sy); update
 # other n
 n_list=[]
 for v in [base_n, base_n+120, base_n-120, 1000, 840, 560]:
   if v%2==0 and 400<=v<=1200 and v not in n_list: n_list.append(v)
 n_done=base_n
 for n_try in n_list[1:]:
   if time.time()>deadline-60: break
   h_int=interpolate_to(best_h,n_try)
   h_s2=make_antisym(h_int,n_try); c=compute_c5(h_s2); update
   h_f2=project_feasible(h_int,n_try); c=compute_c5(h_f2); update
   if time.time()>deadline-80: break
   z2=z_from_h(h_s2,n_try); hz,cz=opt_z(z2,n_try,deadline-50,[30,300,2000,10000]); update
   if time.time()>deadline-70: break
   hf,cf=opt_full(best_h if len(best_h)==n_try else h_s2, n_try, deadline-40, [300,2000], 1500.0); update
 # finalize
 nfin=len(best_h)
 cands=[best_h, project_feasible(best_h,nfin), make_antisym(best_h,nfin)]
 for h in cands:
   if len(h)!=nfin: continue
   if np.all(h>=-1e-9) and np.all(h<=1+1e-9) and abs(float(h.sum())-0.5*nfin)<1e-5:
      c=compute_c5(h)
      if c<best_c: best_h, best_c=h,c
 best_h=np.clip(best_h,0,1)
 # If sum far, project but might change c; do if abs sum>1e-5
 if abs(float(best_h.sum())-0.5*nfin)>1e-5:
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,nfin)
 best_c=compute_c5(best_h)
 return best_h, best_c, nfin
```

```python
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time
import math

def compute_c5(h):
    dx = 2.0 / len(h)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")) * dx)

def interpolate_to(h, n):
    if len(h) == n:
        return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, len(h))
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
    return np.clip(np.interp(x1, x0, h), 0.0, 1.0)

def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.clip(h.copy(), 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * n
    for _ in range(80):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + (diff / tot) * room, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            tot = h.sum()
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + (diff / tot) * h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h

def clean_vec(v, lo, hi):
    v = np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0.0, posinf=hi, neginf=lo)
    return np.clip(v, lo, hi)

def build_from_x(x, n):
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    m = n // 2
    h = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    if n % 2 == 0:
        h[:m] = x
        h[m:] = 1.0 - x[::-1]
    else:
        h[:m] = x
        h[m] = 0.5
        h[m + 1:] = 1.0 - x[::-1]
    return h

def build_antisym(z, n):
    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
    x = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)))
    return build_from_x(x, n)

def make_antisym(h, n):
    m = n // 2
    if n % 2 == 0:
        x = 0.5 * (h[:m] + 1.0 - h[m:][::-1])
    else:
        x = 0.5 * (h[:m] + 1.0 - h[m + 1:][::-1])
    return build_from_x(x, n)

def z_from_h(h, n):
    m = n // 2
    if n % 2 == 0:
        x = 0.5 * (h[:m] + 1.0 - h[m:][::-1])
    else:
        x = 0.5 * (h[:m] + 1.0 - h[m + 1:][::-1])
    x = clean_vec(x, 1e-9, 1.0 - 1e-9)
    return clean_vec(np.log(x / (1.0 - x)), -20.0, 20.0)

def lse_c(c, n, alpha):
    dx = 2.0 / n
    cmax = float(c.max())
    shift = alpha * dx * (c - cmax)
    shift = np.clip(shift, -700.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(shift)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        w = np.zeros(c.shape, dtype=float)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w = e / s
    return float(cmax * dx + math.log(s) / alpha), w

def grad_weights_from_h(h, w, n, fh, N):
    w0 = float(w[n - 1])
    pos = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    neg = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if n > 1:
        pos[1:] = w[n:]
        neg[1:] = w[:n - 1][::-1]
    lin = w0 + np.cumsum(pos) + np.cumsum(neg)[::-1]
    T = pos + neg
    fT = np.fft.rfft(T, N)
    left = np.fft.irfft(fT * fh, N)[:n]
    fTR = np.fft.rfft(T[::-1], N)
    right = np.fft.irfft(fTR * fh, N)[n - 1:n - 1 + n]
    return lin - 2.0 * w0 * h - left - right

def obj_core(h, n, alpha):
    need = 2 * n - 1
    N = 1 << max(1, (need - 1).bit_length())
    h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    fh = np.fft.rfft(h, N)
    v = 1.0 - h
    c = np.fft.irfft(fh * np.fft.rfft(v[::-1], N), N)[:need]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    lse, w = lse_c(c, n, alpha)
    g = grad_weights_from_h(h, w, n, fh, N)
    return lse, w, g

def z_obj(z, n, alpha):
    m = n // 2
    z = clean_vec(z, -50.0, 50.0)
    x = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-z))
    h = build_from_x(x, n)
    lse, _, g = obj_core(h, n, alpha)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    if n % 2 == 0:
        dz = dx * (g[:m] - g[m:][::-1]) * x * (1.0 - x)
    else:
        dz = dx * (g[:m] - g[m + 1:][::-1]) * x * (1.0 - x)
    return float(lse), clean_vec(dz, -1e12, 1e12)

def full_obj(h, n, alpha, rho):
    lse, _, g = obj_core(h, n, alpha)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    diff = float(np.sum(h) - 0.5 * n)
    obj = lse + 0.5 * rho * diff * diff
    grad = dx * g + rho * diff * np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    if not math.isfinite(obj):
        obj = 1.0
    return float(obj), clean_vec(grad, -1e12, 1e12)

def opt_z_lse(z, n, deadline, alphas):
    m = n // 2
    bounds = [(-20.0, 20.0)] * m
    best_h = build_antisym(z, n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h)
    best_z = clean_vec(z, -20.0, 20.0).copy()
    zc = best_z.copy()
    options = {"maxiter": 50, "ftol": 1e-15, "gtol": 1e-9, "maxcor": 20}

    for al in alphas:
        if time.time() > deadline - 3:
            break
        hc = build_antisym(zc, n)
        cc = compute_c5(hc)
        if cc > best_c + 0.002:
            zc = best_z.copy()
        for _ in range(10):
            if time.time() > deadline - 3:
                break
            try:
                res = minimize(z_obj, zc, args=(n, al), jac=True,
                               method="L-BFGS-B", bounds=bounds, options=options)
            except Exception:
                break
            zn = clean_vec(res.x, -20.0, 20.0)
            hn = build_antisym(zn, n)
            cn = compute_c5(hn)
            if cn < best_c:
                best_c = cn
                best_h = hn
                best_z = zn.copy()
            zc = zn
            if res.status == 0 and cn >= best_c - 1e-14:
                break
    return best_h, best_c

def opt_full_penalty(h, n, deadline, alphas, rho):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h)
    hc = h.copy()
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    options = {"maxiter": 50, "ftol": 1e-15, "gtol": 1e-8, "maxcor": 20}

    for al in alphas:
        if time.time() > deadline - 3:
            break
        cc = compute_c5(hc)
        if cc > best_c + 0.002:
            hc = best_h.copy()
        for _ in range(8):
            if time.time() > deadline - 3:
                break
            try:
                res = minimize(full_obj, hc, args=(n, al, rho), jac=True,
                               method="L-BFGS-B", bounds=bounds, options=options)
            except Exception:
                break
            hn = clean_vec(res.x, 0.0, 1.0)
            hn = project_feasible(hn, n)
            cn = compute_c5(hn)
            if cn < best_c:
                best_c = cn
                best_h = hn.copy()
            hc = hn if cn <= best_c + 0.003 else best_h.copy()
            if res.status == 0 and cn >= best_c - 1e-14:
                break
    return best_h, best_c

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    budget = int(max(20, budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + budget - 5
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception:
        h0 = None

    if h0 is None or h0.size < 20:
        n0 = 700
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n0)
        h0 = 0.5 + 0.35 * np.sin(np.pi * x) + 0.15 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x)

    n0 = len(h0)
    base_n = n0 if n0 % 2 == 0 else n0 + 1
    h0 = interpolate_to(h0, base_n)
    h0 = project_feasible(h0, base_n)

    h_sym0 = make_antisym(h0, base_n)
    best_h = h_sym0
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h)
    c_full0 = compute_c5(h0)
    if c_full0 < best_c:
        best_h = h0
        best_c = c_full0

    if time.time() < deadline - 260:
        hz, cz = opt_z_lse(z_from_h(h_sym0, base_n), base_n, deadline - 250,
                           [20.0, 100.0, 600.0, 3000.0, 15000.0])
        if cz < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hz, cz

    hs = make_antisym(best_h, len(best_h))
    cs = compute_c5(hs)
    if cs < best_c:
        best_h, best_c = hs, cs

    if time.time() < deadline - 120:
        hf, cf = opt_full_penalty(best_h, len(best_h), deadline - 100,
                                  [100.0, 500.0, 2500.0, 12000.0], 1500.0)
        if cf < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hf, cf

    hs = make_antisym(best_h, len(best_h))
    cs = compute_c5(hs)
    if cs < best_c:
        best_h, best_c = hs, cs

    n_list = []
    for v in [base_n, base_n + 120, base_n - 120, 1000, 840, 560]:
        if v % 2 == 0 and 400 <= v <= 1200 and v not in n_list:
            n_list.append(v)

    for n_try in n_list[1:]:
        if time.time() > deadline - 70:
            break
        hi = interpolate_to(best_h, n_try)
        hs2 = make_antisym(hi, n_try)
        cs2 = compute_c5(hs2)
        if cs2 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hs2, cs2
        hf2 = project_feasible(hi, n_try)
        cf2 = compute_c5(hf2)
        if cf2 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hf2, cf2

        if time.time() > deadline - 90:
            break
        hz, cz = opt_z_lse(z_from_h(hs2, n_try), n_try, deadline - 80,
                           [40.0, 600.0, 4000.0])
        if cz < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hz, cz

        if time.time() > deadline - 80:
            break
        start = best_h if len(best_h) == n_try else hs2
        hf, cf = opt_full_penalty(start, n_try, deadline - 70,
                                  [500.0, 3000.0], 1500.0)
        if cf < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hf, cf

    while time.time() < deadline - 90:
        ncur = len(best_h)
        pert = best_h + 0.025 * rng.standard_normal(ncur)
        hp = project_feasible(pert, ncur)
        hp = make_antisym(hp, ncur)
        hz, cz = opt_z_lse(z_from_h(hp, ncur), ncur, deadline - 60,
                           [200.0, 2500.0])
        if cz < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hz, cz
        hp = project_feasible(best_h + 0.015 * rng.standard_normal(ncur), ncur)
        hf, cf = opt_full_penalty(hp, ncur, deadline - 60,
                                  [800.0, 5000.0], 2000.0)
        if cf < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hf, cf

    nfin = len(best_h)
    candidates = [
        best_h,
        project_feasible(best_h, nfin),
        make_antisym(best_h, nfin),
    ]
    for hcand in candidates:
        if len(hcand) != nfin:
            continue
        if float(np.min(hcand)) < -1e-12 or float(np.max(hcand)) > 1.0 + 1e-12:
            continue
        if abs(float(hcand.sum()) - 0.5 * nfin) > 1e-5:
            continue
        cc = compute_c5(hcand)
        if cc < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hcand, cc

    best_h = clean_vec(best_h, 0.0, 1.0)
    if abs(float(best_h.sum()) - 0.5 * nfin) > 1e-5:
        best_h = project_feasible(best_h, nfin)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h)
    return best_h, best_c, nfin
```
